We cannot let the only chance
humanity will ever have to
separate money from state pass us by
because of interierence from these
stupid shitcoins. Plenty, and I mean
plenty, of Toxic Bitcoin Maximalists
get it. We have more than enough to
go to war. We have a critical mass of
thought (and memes) more important
than the aggregate user base, actually.
The global collective unconscious is
morphing. We are winning the war of
hearts and minds.
Shitcoins, altcoins,
fiat money, NFTs
and gold are losing.
As Stacy says, you can't have a dicta-
torship and adopt Bitcoin at the same
time. They're axiomatically opposed.
And journalists who suggest that
El Salvador is a "dictatorship" are smo-
king more crack than Hunter Biden. More
on this in a second.
Look at what happened to Peter Schiff.
He tested the Bitcoin Karma Gods (BKG)
and got his ass kicked when regulators
shut down his Puerto Rican bank. His mea
culpas on Twitter are pathetic. He looks
and acts guilty as hell. Even President
Bukele of El Salvador dunked on Schiff.
It's over for this knucklehead. He makes
Hunter Biden look ethical.
The centralizing powers, the powers of
centralization, are losing out to Bitcoin's
dynamic, and this is playing out in coun-
tries around the world at different speeds,
playing in different political theaters to
different audiences.
The world will be better off
on a bitcoin standard, but in
the interim, we still have a
lot of politics in the humanity
stack to work through.

Bitcoin breaks politics, but that doesn't
mean politicians just go away. They'll
keep politicking all the way until
the end. It's what they know. Every
politician is a Boris Johnson sleazebag
ruining their country without hesitation
'til the bitter end. But this time, when
the last politician does the last stupid
political thing, there'll be no one to take
their place because we will all be on a
bitcoin standard.
"Rules, Not Rulers."
Bitcoin has entered its geopolitical
phase, for sure. Some like to think
Bitcoin has nothing to do with politics.
Even brain-dead, hopped-up Hamptons
hedgies that push shitcoins all day -
Looking at you, Novo. This is foolish.
It's like saying Bitcoin has nothing to
do with money. Bitcoin replaces all
forms of money; all the stuff we used
to think of as money, and all the stuil
we think of as money now; mostly fiat
money. But the conversation about
what money is and how it plays a role
in humanity, remains and will always
remain, even after the global bitcoin
standard. Instead of zombie politicians
we will have civil servants paid like
nurses and firefighters. Say goodbye to
moneygrubbing, insider-trading Nancy
Pelosi and her ilk.
Bitcoin replaces politics with a pro-
tocol; a periect governance model that
disintermediates power-mad psychos.
But the actions of various nation-states
as they attempt to deal with Bitcoin
won't stop, and eventually they too will
succumb to Bitcoin. Yes, I already made
this point. So what? I'm recursive. It's
a routine. It's an algorithm. It's code.
It's repetitive. It's God on your 8-track
playing the Bee Gees over and over.
So what? Fuck off.

p2_all_regions:all:all:forward:whole_lines
"You cannot centralize power and
adopt Bitcoin at the same time" - Stacy Herbert
This Tweet from Stacy Herbert is so frickin awesome. It sums up the political debate surrounding
Bitcoin in 11 words. It’s a beauty of concision. A masterpiece of brevity. I can’t stop thinking
about it. I’ll make it the basis of my work in 2022 wherein all shitcoins and nocoiners must die.
We cannot let the only chance
humanity will ever have to
separate money from state pass us by
because of interierence from these
stupid shitcoins. Plenty, and I mean
plenty, of Toxic Bitcoin Maximalists
get it. We have more than enough to
go to war. We have a critical mass of
thought (and memes) more important
than the aggregate user base, actually.
The global collective unconscious is
morphing. We are winning the war of
hearts and minds.
Shitcoins, altcoins,
fiat money, NFTs
and gold are losing.
As Stacy says, you can't have a dicta-
torship and adopt Bitcoin at the same
time. They're axiomatically opposed.
And journalists who suggest that
El Salvador is a "dictatorship" are smo-
king more crack than Hunter Biden. More
on this in a second.
Look at what happened to Peter Schiff.
He tested the Bitcoin Karma Gods (BKG)
and got his ass kicked when regulators
shut down his Puerto Rican bank. His mea
culpas on Twitter are pathetic. He looks
and acts guilty as hell. Even President
Bukele of El Salvador dunked on Schiff.
It's over for this knucklehead. He makes
Hunter Biden look ethical.
The centralizing powers, the powers of
centralization, are losing out to Bitcoin's
dynamic, and this is playing out in coun-
tries around the world at different speeds,
playing in different political theaters to
different audiences.
The world will be better off
on a bitcoin standard, but in
the interim, we still have a
lot of politics in the humanity
stack to work through.
Bitcoin breaks politics, but that doesn't
mean politicians just go away. They'll
keep politicking all the way until
the end. It's what they know. Every
politician is a Boris Johnson sleazebag
ruining their country without hesitation
'til the bitter end. But this time, when
the last politician does the last stupid
political thing, there'll be no one to take
their place because we will all be on a
bitcoin standard.
"Rules, Not Rulers."
Bitcoin has entered its geopolitical
phase, for sure. Some like to think
Bitcoin has nothing to do with politics.
Even brain-dead, hopped-up Hamptons
hedgies that push shitcoins all day -
Looking at you, Novo. This is foolish.
It's like saying Bitcoin has nothing to
do with money. Bitcoin replaces all
forms of money; all the stuff we used
to think of as money, and all the stuil
we think of as money now; mostly fiat
money. But the conversation about
what money is and how it plays a role
in humanity, remains and will always
remain, even after the global bitcoin
standard. Instead of zombie politicians
we will have civil servants paid like
nurses and firefighters. Say goodbye to
moneygrubbing, insider-trading Nancy
Pelosi and her ilk.
Bitcoin replaces politics with a pro-
tocol; a periect governance model that
disintermediates power-mad psychos.
But the actions of various nation-states
as they attempt to deal with Bitcoin
won't stop, and eventually they too will
succumb to Bitcoin. Yes, I already made
this point. So what? I'm recursive. It's
a routine. It's an algorithm. It's code.
It's repetitive. It's God on your 8-track
playing the Bee Gees over and over.
So what? Fuck off.
In a country like El Salvador,
President Bukele has made
bitcoin legal tender fully
understanding that he is
ceding power and political
control to the people.
Having sat down and talked with him a
few times, it’s clear he sees Bitcoin as
a means to liberate his country from all
forms of centralization; central banking.
the IMF, the colonial pressures of the
U.S., the Economic Hitmen, the banana
cartel (read about the United Fruit
Company), and the discombobulating
centralizing psychological pressure
of group-think and propaganda too.
If you're thinking this is not what the
New York Times, Washington Post,
The New Yorker, U.S. State Department,
and various human rights groups
(including those claiming to be closely
aligned with Bitcoin) are reporting,
you're right. They suck. They're all
embarrassingly conflicted and wrong.
Let's get into it.
The media outlets that report on money
and politics but never money in politics,
with their unchecked, unaccounted for
access to trillions in taxes and unlimited
expense accounts, don't want to sud-
denly lose their jobs and their prereq-
uisites to a few thousand lines of code
available freely to the world that allows
people to store and transier wealth in
ways that are virtually free, fast, easy,
unconfiscatable and uncensorable.
So they fucking lie.
Bitcoin is
the meteor.

Bitcoin is the meteor. Nobody wants to be a dinosaur. So they fucking lie.
But they're toast because they are Darwinian-level dangerously stupid,
evolutionarily dead-enders; narcissitic walking anachronisms with shit haircuts.
In the case of instantly irrelevant politicians and instantly unemployable media, the natural tendency is to fight back
and this means ramping up the propaganda along with some of that old school, state-sponsored ultra-violence.
When El Salvador made bitcoin legal
tender it introduced a new era in
Bitcoin's journey (and El Salvador's
journey) stomping on prevailing ialse-
hoods and loudmouth dimwits with
every new block. Bitcoin has its own
internal governance auto-pilot that
obviates the need for any additional
government. As adoption spreads, so
too does the alchemical ability of
Bitcoin to turn greed into altruism
in humans who at their core have an
insatiable desire to acquire bitcoin.
whether they know it or not, thanks to
Satoshi and God. Some call this Jesus
2.0 and I can't argue with that.
But it's wrong to say that politics wont
be part ol the story. As Bitcoin disrupts
all political systems, those living under
these systems will have to live with
depoliticizing their lives. The dinosaurs
had the benefit of getting wiped out
fairly quickly. Our soon-to-be wiped
out politicians will be gasping ior
air during a relatively slow-moving
monetary revolution and they'll have
the weird, unsettling experience of
watching their own death, like Tolstoy's
"The Death of Ivan Ilyich:" the story of
a mostly dead man listening to people
talk shit about him while he's dying,
which provides a messy peak into the
abyss oi death. Are there shitposting
trolls in the afterlife?
So politics has entered
the Bitcoin cage brawl and
here's what we know so far:
El Salvador has a gifted, JFK-meets-
Steve-Jobs President who is truly
Orange-Pilled. The Central Airican
Republic, almost immediately after
making bitcoin legal tender, has got
ten gang-raped by shitcoiners, prin
cipally Sango Coin; a Web3, Silicon
Valley, metaverse con job that will take
CAR from being the second-poorest
country on Earth to dead last. Go long
El Salvador, short CAR.
It should be noted that El Salvador
has a task force keeping shitcoins
and NFTs out ol the country. Already,
several high-prolile shitcoiners and
Ponzi freaks have been tossed out.
Shitcoins like EOS, Cardano, XRP, and
Ethereum are effectively banned. Or
more precisely, only Bitcoin is legal and
everything else, per the new security
laws, will have to pass through regu-
latory hoops. At the moment, speaking
only for myself, there isn't a single
shitcoin that would get through this
process and it's highly unlikely any
ever will. Terrorism must be deieated,
including shitcoin terrorism.
Shitcoiners are having a tough time
these days, and I don't feel sorry for
them. They're scum. Their projects
were always gonna crash and burn.
They are cotton candy machines spin-
ning gossamer fraud served up to inno-
cent children hoping for "yield" and
Bitcoin-like 10,000x returns. When you
see a shitcoiner, think of the clowns at
kids' parties that moonlight as serial
killers. That's who shitcoiners are.
Ponzi promoters, exit-scammers, and
various cult leaders all having their
moment of fame on the market capi-
talization table before dumping.
Shitcoining is a disease
like alcoholism and
drug addiction.
Those who shitcoin need a 12-step
recovery program. Shitcoiners-

help them recover from their disease.

start one. Those who shitcoin need a

tender it introduced a new era in

journey) stomping on prevailing ialse-

insatiable desire to acquire bitcoin.
whether they know it or not, thanks to

countries in Latin America. But that
doesn’t help the Gringo psychos ben-

The GDP numbers are up. And the trend is positive.
All the data quoted in the Times piece is wrong.
What about Bitcoin?
#FACT:
The entire country is aware of Bitcoin. Wherever I go,
everybody knows about it. Adoption is one of the highest in
the world, and growing. The idea of individual sovereignty
and unconfiscatable, uncensorable money has caught
on with the population, who have known for too long the
financial rape and pillage from America, and they want out.
#FACT:

millions of dollars, and growing fast. The country will save
$400 million a year in fees using bitcoin.

weasel his way into El Salvador with his shitcoins, but was
shown the door. As mentioned, all the shitcoiners have
been told they are not welcome in El Salvador. It should be
noted that there is no such thing as the Bitcoin Foundation
of which Brock claims to be chairman. It was, and still is,
a stillborn mess.
#FACT:
The flow of emigration is reversing and Salvadorans living
in the U.S. and other countries for years are moving back.
Additionally, the New York Times
piece prints a horrific lie about
child abuse in their story that can't
be repeated; it's too heinous. But
what I will say is that it's a case of
the pot-calling-the-kettle black as
the incidence of American children
getting murdered at school and/
or getting adult "drag" performers
exposing their genitalia to them for
"entertainment" has gone exponen-
tial. America needs to start thinking
about saving its own children first
before opining about other countries.
Finally, the New York Times doesn't
know squat about Bitcoin. The piece
tries to square the circle regarding
Bitcoin "ethos" and the realpolitik of
what's happening in El Salvador, and
it fails miserably. It’s just more fiat-
thinking propaganda from the New York
Times; a paper whose most famous
columnist Paul Krugman once said the
quiet part out loud when he spilled the
beans publicly and confessed the U.S
dollar is backed with nothing but mur-
derous state violence. What a dick.

"You cannot centralize power and
adopt Bitcoin at the same time" - Stacy Herbert
This Tweet from Stacy Herbert is so frickin awesome. It sums up the political debate surrounding
Bitcoin in 11 words. It’s a beauty of concision. A masterpiece of brevity. I can’t stop thinking
about it. I’ll make it the basis of my work in 2022 wherein all shitcoins and nocoiners must die.
We cannot let the only chance
humanity will ever have to
separate money from state pass us by
because of interierence from these
stupid shitcoins. Plenty, and I mean
plenty, of Toxic Bitcoin Maximalists
get it. We have more than enough to
go to war. We have a critical mass of
thought (and memes) more important
than the aggregate user base, actually.
The global collective unconscious is
morphing. We are winning the war of
hearts and minds.
Shitcoins, altcoins,
fiat money, NFTs
and gold are losing.
As Stacy says, you can't have a dicta-
torship and adopt Bitcoin at the same
time. They're axiomatically opposed.
And journalists who suggest that
El Salvador is a "dictatorship" are smo-
king more crack than Hunter Biden. More
on this in a second.
Look at what happened to Peter Schiff.
He tested the Bitcoin Karma Gods (BKG)
and got his ass kicked when regulators
shut down his Puerto Rican bank. His mea
culpas on Twitter are pathetic. He looks
and acts guilty as hell. Even President
Bukele of El Salvador dunked on Schiff.
It's over for this knucklehead. He makes
Hunter Biden look ethical.
The centralizing powers, the powers of
centralization, are losing out to Bitcoin's
dynamic, and this is playing out in coun-
tries around the world at different speeds,
playing in different political theaters to
different audiences.
The world will be better off
on a bitcoin standard, but in
the interim, we still have a
lot of politics in the humanity
stack to work through.
Bitcoin breaks politics, but that doesn't
mean politicians just go away. They'll
keep politicking all the way until
the end. It's what they know. Every
politician is a Boris Johnson sleazebag
ruining their country without hesitation
'til the bitter end. But this time, when
the last politician does the last stupid
political thing, there'll be no one to take
their place because we will all be on a
bitcoin standard.
"Rules, Not Rulers."
Bitcoin has entered its geopolitical
phase, for sure. Some like to think
Bitcoin has nothing to do with politics.
Even brain-dead, hopped-up Hamptons
hedgies that push shitcoins all day -
Looking at you, Novo. This is foolish.
It's like saying Bitcoin has nothing to
do with money. Bitcoin replaces all
forms of money; all the stuff we used
to think of as money, and all the stuil
we think of as money now; mostly fiat
money. But the conversation about
what money is and how it plays a role
in humanity, remains and will always
remain, even after the global bitcoin
standard. Instead of zombie politicians
we will have civil servants paid like
nurses and firefighters. Say goodbye to
moneygrubbing, insider-trading Nancy
Pelosi and her ilk.
Bitcoin replaces politics with a pro-
tocol; a periect governance model that
disintermediates power-mad psychos.
But the actions of various nation-states
as they attempt to deal with Bitcoin
won't stop, and eventually they too will
succumb to Bitcoin. Yes, I already made
this point. So what? I'm recursive. It's
a routine. It's an algorithm. It's code.
It's repetitive. It's God on your 8-track
playing the Bee Gees over and over.
So what? Fuck off.

President Bukele has made
bitcoin legal tender fully
understanding that he is
ceding power and political

a means to liberate his country from all
forms of centralization; central banking.
the IMF, the colonial pressures of the

The New Yorker, U.S. State Department,
and various human rights groups

aligned with Bitcoin) are reporting,
you're right. They suck. They're all
embarrassingly conflicted and wrong.

The media outlets that report on money
and politics but never money in politics,
with their unchecked, unaccounted for
access to trillions in taxes and unlimited
expense accounts, don't want to sud-
denly lose their jobs and their prereq-
uisites to a few thousand lines of code
available freely to the world that allows
people to store and transier wealth in
ways that are virtually free, fast, easy,
unconfiscatable and uncensorable.
So they fucking lie.
Bitcoin is
the meteor.
